Um, yeah, I do get a bit tired. I mean, I really don't want to complain, because I know everyone gets worn out after a long day at work, right? It's probably totally normal. But... well, I guess lately, by the time I get home from the office, even just the really simple things feel like a lot. 

Like, just walking to the end of the driveway to check the mail, or... or putting a few plates in the dishwasher. It just feels like way more effort than it really should be. I still get it done, mostly! I don't want you to think I'm just sitting around doing nothing. It just takes a bit more out of me than usual, I guess. Is that... is that helpful? I hope I'm not just whining about normal everyday stuff.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Um... well, I really try to keep a positive attitude, you know? I don't want to be someone who just sits around feeling sorry for myself, especially when there's no real reason for it. 

But... I guess, if I'm being completely honest... yeah. Most days lately, I've just had this... this heavy, low feeling. It's hard to explain, but it's kind of like I'm just moving through work and home, but... like the color has been turned down on everything? 

I really hope that makes sense. I know I shouldn't complain when nothing is actually going wrong in my life, but it just sort of hangs around in the background almost all day. I'm trying my best to push through it, though! Is that... does that help with your assessment? I just want to make sure I'm explaining it the right way for you.
